### Post #1: 2024-08-06 16:12 — `$IWM, $SPY, $QQQ`

**USD/JPY - $SPY $QQQ $IWM**

Please everyone use caution lol. I understand FOMO and buy the dip. The reality of the issue is still unfolding - and no, I'm not saying a recession.

I'm just talking about how the USD/JPY situation will be playing out for awhile. There was more than a decade of very low interest out of Japan. And now it's over and this is still very early... the reverse carry trade is in place now. There is a correlation between strength is USD and stock market rally. So any pullbacks in Yen will rally stocks short term.  Any strength in Yen or USD weakness will cause pullbacks. The goal of Japan was literally to strengthen a weakened Yen.

My guess is short rallies over next couple months, and another pullback into Oct/Nov

### Post #20: 2024-11-14 01:15 — `$SMCI` `[Discuss]`

**$SMCI - Worth the Gamble?**

Obviously there is a selloff of $SMCI for very valid reasons, but doesn't it appear pretty juicy right now? If they file their plan to regain compliance by Nov 16th, and get their shit together... I think it could 2x-5x easily. Alternatively, they could remain uncompliant and be delisted which would send the stock to single digits or below. What do you think?

Here are some Pros and Cons

Pros:

	1.	Growth from AI Demand: SMCI provides high-performance computing solutions, a space benefiting from the rapid rise in AI applications. As companies build out their AI infrastructure, SMCI’s servers are in high demand, positioning it well for growth.
	2.	Strong Revenue and Earnings Growth: SMCI has consistently reported strong revenue and earnings, supported by rising demand for high-performance servers. This growth in financial performance has made the stock attractive to investors focused on fundamentals.
	3.	Competitive Niche: SMCI’s customization options for clients allow it to differentiate itself from larger competitors. This focus on niche, high-performance computing solutions has given SMCI a foothold with companies that want tailored server solutions.
	4.	Reasonable Valuation: Despite its growth potential, SMCI is often valued at a lower multiple than AI giants like Nvidia, which may appeal to investors looking for AI exposure at a discount.
	5.	Potential for Market Upside: Given the broader trend of AI-driven computing, SMCI could still see further stock gains, especially if it continues winning new contracts or partnerships in the AI sector.

Cons:

	1.	Failure to File 10-K on Time: A recent red flag is SMCI’s failure to file its 10-K on time, which raises concerns over its internal controls and financial transparency. Delayed filings can signal potential accounting or regulatory issues and may lead to penalties, impacting investor confidence and causing stock volatility.
	2.	Competitive Pressure: SMCI faces intense competition from larger players like Dell and HP in the server market. These companies have greater resources, potentially putting pressure on SMCI’s market share and profit margins.
	3.	AI Dependency: While AI-related demand is driving SMCI’s growth, any slowdown or change in demand could negatively affect it. If the AI build-out slows down, SMCI’s revenue growth might taper off.
	4.	Supply Chain and Production Risks: SMCI relies on complex global supply chains. Disruptions in component availability or geopolitical issues could slow its production, impacting its ability to meet demand or maintain pricing.
	5.	Increased Stock Volatility: SMCI’s price has been quite volatile, partly due to its high valuation and speculative appeal as an AI-related stock. Any negative news, especially regarding financial filings, could lead to sharp sell-offs, which may not suit conservative investors.
	

*Link: https://afterhour.com/ThetaMaestro/kUg/dollarsmci-worth-the-gamble*

**$RXRX - Update Call on Nov 20th for merge with Exscientia $EXAI**

Voters for both $EXAI (Exscientia) and $RXRX (Recursion) voted to approve the proposed company combination. On Nov 20th, Recursion will be hosting a call to discuss this merge as it's set to close on November 20, 2024, subject to remaining regulatory approvals. 

In anticipation, I wanted to highlight some of the benefits of this combination:

-Combination of Recursion’s expertise in biological exploration and chemical mapping capabilities with Exscientia’s expertise in AI-driven molecular design and automated chemical synthesis, creating a comprehensive drug discovery platform

-Expanded therapeutic reach including oncology, neurology, and rare diseases. The integration will strengthen data-driven research, including one of the largest proprietary datasets for biological and chemical analysis.

-The partnership will accelerate the development of new medicines, leveraging both companies’ technologies to streamline and scale drug discovery.

-The merged company will reduce costs associated with drug development through shared infrastructure and AI-driven efficiencies

-Combined they plan to initiate approximately ten clinical trials by the end of 2025, reflecting a significant ramp-up in its pipeline. They also have 850M combined cash

-The merger enhances the companies’ global reach, with facilities in key locations such as the U.S., UK, and Europe
	
-The AI Leader in Pharma... The deal positions the combined company as a leader in AI-driven pharmaceutical innovation, aiming to redefine the economics and timelines of drug development.

### Post #24: 2024-11-18 21:43 — `$RXRX, $EXAI` `[Dd]`

**Recursion - Using AI for Trial Development $RXRX**

TLDR; They are also using AI to plan clinical trials, which saves time/money (usually 4-6 months which is down to 4-6 weeks)


We all know $RXRX is using AI for drug discovery and to map out biology. However here's one more benefit to consider... They are using AI to help execute the trial development and cost. 

Here's are a few examples according to their recent earnings call, featuring the CEO of $EXAI  (https://www.youtube.com/live/0lm6zBzpcjw?si=P2alabuBAiv0TGQo)

80% of the time cost of a trial is "trial development", which is broken down into two main parts:


1. Clinical Trial Design
2. Trial Execution 

Clinical Trial Design - Overall design, which patients to treat, inclusion / exclusion criteria, etc.

Trial Execution - Selection a location, understanding where eligible patients are, how to select them, data monitoring, etc.

Recursion is using AI including real and multi-modal data to build the trial more effectively. They use this to understand where the patients are, exactly who's eligible, etc. For example, the latest cohort was found in 4-6 weeks vs 4-6 months which is the industry standard.

This is a huge save on time and money for their upcoming trials. In addition, I could see this being an offering as an additional service to existing pharma companies at a cost.

Make sure to check out more $RXRX DD on @squeezekid page!

### Post #32: 2024-11-22 19:48 — `$RXRX, $EXAI` `[Dd]`

**$RXRX - Next Catalyst? The Readouts...**

I don't expect much share price movement until we have some of the upcoming clinical trial readouts....

As a reminder, $RXRX and  $EXAI have merged, with Exscientia now operating as a wholly owned subsidiary of Recursion.

Here's the recap of upcoming readouts ⬇️⬇️⬇️

💉REC-617 (CDK7 inhibitor for advanced solid tumors): Initial Phase 1 monotherapy safety and pharmacokinetics/pharmacodynamics data are expected at the AACR Special Conference on December 9, 2024, followed by a webinar on December 10, 2024.

💉REC-4881 (MEK1/2 inhibitor for Familial Adenomatous Polyposis): Phase 1b/2 safety and preliminary efficacy data are anticipated in the first half of 2025.

💉REC-2282 (pan-HDAC inhibitor for Neurofibromatosis Type 2): A progression-free survival at 6 months (PFS6) futility analysis is expected by the first half of 2025.

💉REC-3565 (MALT1 inhibitor for B-cell malignancies): The first patient dosing in Phase 1 is expected in the first quarter of 2025.

💉REC-4539 (LSD1 inhibitor for small-cell lung cancer): The first patient dosing in Phase 1 is expected in the first half of 2025.

💉REC-994 (superoxide scavenger for Cerebral Cavernous Malformation): Further data are to be shared at an upcoming medical conference or publication in the first half of 2025, with a regulatory update expected by the second half of 2025.

💉REC-394 (C. difficile Toxin B selective inhibitor for C. difficile infection): A Phase 2 update is expected in the first quarter of 2026.

💉REC-1245 (RBM39 degrader for solid tumors and lymphoma): Phase 1 dose-escalation data are expected in the first half of 2026.

### Post #35: 2024-11-24 17:36 — `$WMT` `[Discuss]`

**Struggling With Options? Try This ⬇️**

I made a comment on another post and thought this could help anyone who is new to options trading. Most people end up losing money, so don't be one of them! 💰💰

The number one killer of option premium is THETA (time decay)⏳. As you get closer to the option expiration, Theta accelerates at a much greater rate. Your goal is to increase your chances of making a profit, while minimizing Theta.

First, yes you can make money doing 0DTE calls, but most don't and that's not my strategy. Feel free to win the lottery or blow up your account doing that strategy!

✅ Here are the basic rules I have for swing trading options (see my example trades at the bottom - please note spot prices are wrong on many so I'm not down 99%):

1. Call Strike: Always ITM or ATM calls. Avoid way OTM calls, they are cheap because they expire worthless 99% of time. If you're doing a LEAP (option expiring 1+ year from now), it's ok to go slightly OTM, but only slightly and Theta doesn't hurt you hardly at all.

2. Call Expiration: At least Monthly calls or longer, preferably always 90+ days out. Avoid daily or weekly calls - Statistically your chances of losses are much higher a day out vs. a year out (see image). Theta accelerates within 30 days to expiration. You pay a premium for time, and what's the #1 killer of options? Theta (time decay)

3. Closing Position: Never let an option expire worthless. Cut it early at a loss if momentum is shifting against you. Inversely, let a position run for as long as it has momentum. If within 30 days to expiration, consider cutting before Theta increases UNLESS there is a major catalyst like Earnings, Event, etc before it expires (risk vs reward really needs to be analyzed).

Yes, you'll pay more for the types of contracts... but you'll make money at a much higher percentage (some money is better than no money). 

Here are some examples of a few trades that I did so you can see how it works (see my posts or current position for proof).

$WMT -  It's August 2024, WMT share price is $68 and earnings coming up. I bought $70C for $3.18 a contract, Exp 12/20, and bought 50 of them. Earnings went well, price jumped to $75 and I was up 35k within 10 days. I could have sold but guidance was great and I felt stock could keep running. It went up to 85 over next months, consolidated near 82 before Nov earnings. At this point, I'm up 55k and getting within 30 days. I decided to let it ride, earnings crushed again and it went up to 88 a share, and I cashed out at 73k profit. I should have held because its 90 (another 10k profit) but I needed to lock in profits.

$NVDA - Its August 2024, the share price is 126. I decide to do $129 Strike with June 2025 expiration. These cost more but you'll see why it's worth it. Aug earnings are great but stock sells off, drops to almost $100 by Sept... So if I had Weekly calls, I'd lose all 50k I invested in options. Of course stock bounces back, hits 149 by November, but my expiration is still very far out (theta minimal). So Nov earnings crush, and stock is consolidating still. I still have this open but you don't think this will be 150+ by March? I'm still up 12k and NVDA will crush.

You don't know how a stock momentum will shift or how earnings will impact a stock. But if you have more time, you have a much better chance or being profitable if it's a good company.

Did this help? Please give me a like and follow 🙏🏻

*Link: https://afterhour.com/ThetaMaestro/mqV/struggling-with-options-try-th*

**Taking the $UBER Options Ride**

In the coming days/weeks, I'm going to be buying a series of ATM options (probably $75 strike) with various expirations from March - June 2025. I plan to buy roughly $100K in options, which I believe will have a 3X return.

$UBER recently took a hit because of concerns about $TSLA autonomous fleet on the horizon, and slightly slower bookings growth and adjusted EBITDA guidance (despite being in line with consensus at the midpoint of the guide)

Let's take a look at how they're positioned: 

🎯 RECENT EARNINGS:

✅ EPS: $1.20 vs. 41 cents expected
✅ Revenue: $11.19B vs. $10.98B

They absolutely crushed earnings and grew revenue by 20%.

🎯ANALYST FORECAST

✅ Minimum price target is $75 
✅ Highest is $120

Out of 56 analyst, there are 45 Strong Buy, 5 Buy and 6 Hold. Not a single sell or strong sell.

🎯CATALYST:

1.	Massive Growth Potential in Mobility and Delivery:

▫️Uber dominates the ride-hailing market in many parts of the world. Its diversified revenue streams (rides, Uber Eats, freight logistics) create a strong, scalable business model. Post-pandemic trends show continued recovery in mobility demand, alongside booming food delivery.

▫️Emerging markets like India and Southeast Asia provide opportunities for expansion where the competition is less consolidated.

2.	Focus on Profitability:

▫️Uber has shifted its strategy from rapid growth to disciplined profitability. Recent earnings reports show improving margins and the company’s ability to generate positive free cash flow consistently—a key indicator of financial health.

3.	Platform Ecosystem:

▫️Uber’s “super app” model integrates rides, delivery, and even public transit options, creating a one-stop-shop for urban mobility. This diversified approach fosters customer loyalty and cross-selling opportunities, reducing customer acquisition costs.

4.	Network Effects and Scale:

▫️Uber’s vast network of drivers and users is a significant competitive moat. The more riders and drivers on the platform, the more efficient the matching process, reducing wait times and costs for all parties.

5.	Autonomous Vehicle (AV) Readiness Without Risk Overexposure:

▫️While Tesla invests heavily in developing its autonomous driving technology, Uber has outsourced much of this risk, partnering with Aurora for AV technology. If successful, Uber can deploy self-driving vehicles at scale without the R&D costs that Tesla bears.

There are always risks, however my bet is that $UBER will have a strong recovery and hit at least $85 by spring.

### Post #49: 2024-11-30 19:27 — `$CAVA, $SPY, $SBUX` `[Discuss]`

**🎯 The Power of Consistency 📈**

We focus a lot on YOLO / trading here (I'm an Options swing trader, after all). However, you can guarantee wealth by investing consistently in the long-term.

Don't believe me? Let's take a look at the numbers:

💰If you invest $250 monthly in $SPY for 30 years, reinvesting dividends and assuming an average annual return of 10%, here’s what you’d have at the end:

	•	Total Value: $515,711
	•	Total Contributions: $90,000
	•	Compound Interest Earned: $425,711

💰💰What if you doubled it and could afford $500 a month?

	•	Total Value: $1,031,422
	•	Total Contributions: $180,000
	•	Compound Interest Earned: $851,422

❌ BUT... "I can't afford to invest any money!!"

Shut up! You're lying to yourself while you pay for that $SBUX drink that cost you $8, and then head to $CAVA for lunch to spend another $12. You either need to find ways to spend less (like taking that $20 above and investing weekly), or find ways to make more money. But you need to consistently invest and focus on the long-term.

Let's get real now, because I know we're all degenerates who love to trade. 

Here are some tips that helped me:

✅ Set a limit you can afford to invest in $SPY or similar S&P ETFs each week. Have it invest automatically (Robinhood and other platforms make this very easy). Only touch it to increase the amount as you start to earn more, but make a commitment to yourself to never cancel it!

✅ Set aside money you can reasonably afford to trade, if you must. Until you have a reasonable $SPY nest egg, agree to take the profits from the trade (or a portion of them) and add it to your long-term.

✅ Don't worry about timing the market - A study by Charles Schwab shows that over a 20-year period, an investor using DCA outperformed someone sitting in cash and waiting for the “perfect time” to invest. Weekly deposits is what I do.

✅ The earlier you start, the better! See the image I've attached - the multiplier of your 💵 is much greater at an earlier age (Wealth Multiplier). Start investing for you kids too to maximize the power of every $1 today.

Start small, stay consistent, and let time in the market work its magic! 🤑

Was this helpful? Consider liking and following for more and check out my Options Trading post for tips on that. 🙏🏼

*Link: https://afterhour.com/ThetaMaestro/nlK/the-power-of-consistency*

### Post #60: 2024-12-04 07:38 `[Discuss]`

**🚀 Boost Your Credit Score to 850! 💳**

This isn't stock related, but I worked in Finance for 15 years and figured I can pass along some tips on increasing your credit score.

First, remember there are three primary credit bureaus: Equifax, Experian, and TransUnion.


WHY is having a great credit score important?


✅ Lower rates = big savings on mortgages, car loans, and credit cards.
✅ Easier Loan Approvals: Lenders are more likely to say yes when you have a strong credit history.
✅ Better Credit Card Perks: Higher limits, lower fees, and exclusive rewards come with higher scores.
✅ Housing & Jobs: Landlords and even some employers check your credit to gauge responsibility.


HOW do I get that 8️⃣5️⃣0️⃣ score?


1️⃣ Pay On Time, Every Time: Late payments will hurt your score the most
2️⃣ Keep Credit Utilization Low: Aim to use less than 10% of your credit limit. Even if you pay your credit cards off in full each month, do NOT let it close with a balance above 10% your credit limit. Example - credit limit is 1,000 and you've spent 900, pay off 800 or more before the statement dare closes
3️⃣ Lengthen Credit History: Keep old accounts open to show a solid track record. Avoid opening too many new ones, and avoid closing them. I have my first card from when I was 18, which I use once a year to keep it active (no cash back)
4️⃣ Diversify Credit Mix: A blend of credit cards, loans, and mortgages shows you can handle various types of credit. Having 3 credit cards isn't a mix - but a credit card, home loan and auto loan... 😍
5️⃣ Check Your Reports: Errors can cost you points - see tip below on how to check.

BONUS TIPS

🎯 You can Freeze your credit (usually for free) with all 3 credit bureaus, and then "thaw" it when getting a new loan. This helps stop any identity theft

🎯 Most banks offer a free monthly FICO report, such as the one in my image

🎯 You can get a free credit report from the official website AnnualCreditReport.com. It’s authorized by federal law and allows you to check your credit reports from the three major bureaus—Equifax, Experian, and TransUnion—once a year for free.

**Let Your Winners Run 🏃🏃**

Successful trading is about letting your winners run and cutting your losses quickly. 

I recently sold $WMT, making 73K in profit when it hit around $88 a share. Others took the same trade, but exited much sooner when it was $75. I felt that I had really outsmarted them, and followed my mantra of "Letting your winners run".

Reason for selling? P/E is getting too big for box store, and price has run way too much. It's the same reason people try and short a stock when it's run up, thinking it just simply can't go higher. It can. And it does.

But today Walmart is above $95. I had 50 contracts, which would have been another profit of 40K just two weeks later.

There is no shame in taking profits... I'm proud of my trade and taking it from 17K to 90K. This is to reflect that we can always learn. Momentum had not stopped, and that's my #1 rule.

In the amazing book Best Losers Win, one of the greatest traders Tom Hougaard emphasizes the power of patience—holding onto winning trades even when fear tempts you to lock in profits. Big gains often come from staying in the game longer than others. Don’t cap your upside; let your winners do the heavy lifting!

### Post #85: 2024-12-08 18:33 `[Dd]`

**Art of Covered Calls: Profit Beyond Stock Price 🚀**

Investing isn’t just about buying low and selling high—smart strategies can help you profit even when stock prices dip. 

💡Take $ASTS, for example. I bought it at $27.50, and the price has since gone down. But guess what? I’m still profitable!

How? By consistently selling covered calls. 📈

🎯 Covered calls let you collect premium income while holding the stock, offsetting losses and creating a steady cash flow. It’s like getting paid to wait for a rebound!

As of Friday, I'm down $1,300 on my shares however I've collected $6,570 in covered calls premium since September.

❌ But what happens if your strike price gets hit and you fear the shares will be taken?  You have 3  Options:

1️⃣ Let the Calls Get Exercised

Outcome: Your shares are sold at the strike price, locking in profits (or limiting losses) from the initial trade.

When to Choose: If you’re happy with the strike price and ready to part with the shares.

2️⃣ Buy Back the Calls

Outcome: Close your position by repurchasing the calls, typically at a higher price than the premium you sold them for.

When to Choose: If you want to keep the shares because you expect further upside. This avoids assignment but may reduce overall profits.

3️⃣ Roll the Options Forward

Outcome: Close the current calls (buy them back) and sell new calls with a later expiration and/or higher strike price.

When to Choose: If you want to maintain the strategy, keep your shares, and capture more premium income while giving the stock more room to grow.

Each option has its own risk and reward trade-offs, so the best choice depends on your goals and market outlook.

This strategy isn’t just about surviving market downturns—it’s about thriving in them. 💰


*Link: https://afterhour.com/ThetaMaestro/oYc/art-of-covered-calls-profit-be*

### Post #86: 2024-12-09 02:52 `[Discuss]`

**Estate Planning - Prepare for the Worst**

As requested by @moonopoly!  

⚠️ Disclaimer: I am not an attorney or Estate Planner, I'm just sharing my experience. Talk to an estate planning attorney for your state and personal specifics.

🏡 Nobody likes to think about death, but planning ahead ensures your loved ones are cared for and your legacy is protected. One of the most effective tools for estate planning is a revocable trust, and here’s why:

🔹 What is a Revocable Trust?
It’s a legal document that holds your assets during your lifetime and distributes them according to your wishes after you’re gone, without going through probate. 

Probate is basically where if you have assets / wealth over a certain threshold (amount varies by state), it then gets assigned to State, whom then makes decisions on where your assets go. Without a trust, your assets could go to undesired heirs and they also charges hefty fees.

🔹 Why Do You Need A Trust?

• Avoid Probate: Save your loved ones time and money by skipping the court process.
• Privacy: Unlike a will, a trust is not a public record.
• Flexibility: You can change it anytime while you’re alive.
• Control: Outline how and when your assets are distributed, divided amongst children, family and relatives. 

🔹 What’s Included in a Trust?

1. Trustee: Manages the trust’s assets during your lifetime (often yourself).
2. Successor Trustee: Takes over management and distributes assets after your incapacity or death.
3. Beneficiary: The person(s) or organization(s) receiving the assets.
4. Assets: Property, accounts, and items placed in the trust for beneficiaries.

🔹 What Does It Cost?

Setting up a revocable trust typically costs $1,500 to $3,500 in the U.S., depending on your state and the complexity of your estate. While it’s an investment upfront, it can save your family thousands in probate fees later and a headache.

🔹 Other Key Documents for Estate Planning:

These are additional estate documents that are often included in your Estate Planning cost, but can be very beneficial:

• Durable Power of Attorney (for finances)
• Healthcare Directive (your medical wishes)
• Last Will (for items not covered in the trust)

🔹 Other Key Estate Recommendations

• Obtain a Term Life Insurance policy for you and your significant other. Term Life pays a set amount and you have the protection for a specified term (1 Million Policy | 30 Year Term is very common). This gives you peace of mind that your beneficiaries have what they need if you were to pass. You can designate your Trust as the Beneficiary

• Ensure all your assets are in your Trust. It's not enough to just get a Trust, you must change the ownership of your bank/investment accounts, vehicles, properties, etc to your Trust. Any that you can't, ensure the POD (Pay on Death) or Beneficiary is your Trust.

• Include a list of where your accounts are at, Safe Deposit Box, Assets, etc in the front page of your Trust. Update it yearly. This will help your Successor Trustee greatly when they reconcile your assets and get your beneficiaries paid.

💬 Have questions or want to share your experience with estate planning? Drop a comment below!

*Link: https://afterhour.com/ThetaMaestro/o2d/estate-planning-prepare-for-th*

### Post #95: 2024-12-10 04:15 `[Dd]`

**🎄📈 December Seasonality: What’s Happening Now?**


As we approach mid-December, the market’s seasonality shines through - it's not just about the “Santa Claus rally.” 

Around this time, hedge funds and institutional investors are busy rebalancing their portfolios before year-end. Here’s why this matters:

1️⃣ Tax-Loss Harvesting: Funds may sell off underperforming assets to offset gains, creating short-term volatility in specific stocks.

2️⃣ Repositioning for 2024: Many institutions are shifting capital into sectors or assets they believe will outperform in the new year.

3️⃣ Liquidity Adjustments: December tends to see lighter volume, making these moves more pronounced.

Historically, the back half of December has been a sweet spot for markets. Post-rebalancing flows, combined with retail investors and mutual fund activity, often spark a strong finish to the year. See the attached images.

👉 As a swing trader, watch for opportunities in beaten-down stocks that might recover after fund selling subsides. 

The markets are setting the stage—how are you positioning for the final weeks of 2024? Let me know your thoughts below!

*Link: https://afterhour.com/ThetaMaestro/pjy/december-seasonality-whats-hap*

Former $UBER CBO discusses how some recent Uber partnerships, like the $TOST one, will help them win in the long-term. 

🚀 "I think you'll see in Q1 a recovery back into the 70s and 80s because their profit are coming faster than predicted a couple years ago. They talked about getting 5B in free cash flow by 2026 or 2027, and they're gonna beat that now while growing and possibly entering the grocery delivery space"

1️⃣ Market Dominance: Uber leads the ride-sharing industry with pricing power and driver appeal, cementing its edge over competitors like Lyft.

2️⃣ Expanding Horizons: The potential acquisition of Instacart could bolster Uber’s grocery delivery presence, strengthening its diversified growth.

3️⃣ Facing Challenges: Autonomous vehicle advancements (like Waymo’s) present a long-term challenge. Uber mitigates risks by partnering with AV leaders.

### Post #106: 2024-12-11 22:05 — `$UBER` `[News]`

**🚗💡 Key Takeaways from $UBER Barclays Tech Conference:**

In case you missed it today, the CFO of $UBER, Prashanth Mahendra-Rajah, did a fireside chat at the Barclays Tech Conference.

Here are the recaps of my notes 

TLDR; I'm bullish AF! 🚀

1️⃣ Autonomous Vehicles (AVs):

• Uber sees AVs as a key solution to address supply constraints in both developed and developing markets.
• Add your AV to Uber’s fleet, and they handle the rest, allowing you to earn money at a competitive take rate.
• Why Uber is the best partner for AV operators:

1. Demand aggregation: Hundreds of millions of app users, growing 15% annually.
2. Advanced routing and mapping technology to optimize trips and earnings.
3. Operational excellence: Rider ID, payments, fraud prevention, and customer service—all handled seamlessly.

2️⃣ Expanding AV Partnerships:

• Strengthened relationship with Waymo: Austin and Atlanta added to the successful pilot in Phoenix.
• Moove partnership in Miami: Uber’s fleet OS is evolving, positioning Moove to optimize fleet operations.

3️⃣ International Robotaxi Expansion:

• Ready to deploy in the Middle East by 2025; plans for Europe are in the works despite regulatory hurdles.

4️⃣ Strong Growth in Mobility & Delivery:

• Mobility business projected to grow high teens to low 20s CAGR through 2025, supported by new products and insurance savings initiatives.
• Delivery expansion: Grocery business growing fast; partnerships like Instacart drive usage.

5️⃣ Uber One Membership Program:

• 25M members, up 70%, with plans to expand beyond the current 20 countries.
• One users drive more profitability and use more Uber products

💬 Uber’s CFO emphasized their unique position in AV deployment, fleet optimization, and global expansion while delivering consistent growth for investors.

📈 Uber’s future is bright with innovation, strategic partnerships, and expanding markets leading the way.

### Post #122: 2024-12-14 15:55 — `$SPY` `[Dd]`

**Market Breadth... Concerning?**

The S&P 500 ($SPY) just had 9 consecutive sessions with negative breadth. That's happened fewer than 20 times in 70 years. 

❓Should we be concerned?

📉 What is Negative Market Breadth: This occurs when most stocks declines, even if major indices like the S&P 500 appear stable or are rising. This divergence often suggests that gains are concentrated in a few large-cap stocks, while the majority of stocks are underperforming.

⚠️ Is It Concerning: While weak breadth continues, in the context of rising moving averages and a bull market, history shows that similar conditions led to a +3.1% median S&P 500 gain over the month.

🔥 Inflation Concerns: Unlike 2022, inflation worries are less severe, with charts showing growth outperforming value. A bullish inverse head-and-shoulders pattern could be forming. There are no current signs that markets are turning over.

### Post #125: 2024-12-15 16:15 — `$NVDA, $JNJ, $PLTR, $IWM, $KO, $AVGO, $TSLA` `[Dd]`

**🔥 Top Trading Strategies for Newbies**


1️⃣ Sector Rotation

Focus: Ride shifts in sectors based on the economy.
Example: Tech in growth cycles, utilities during recessions.

2️⃣ Thematic Investing

Focus: Megatrends like AI, EVs, or clean energy
Example: EV giants like $TSLA or AI leaders like $NVDA, $PLTR and $AVGO

3️⃣ Momentum Trading

Focus: Trade stocks with strong trends upwards or downwards
Tip: Watch out for reversals, exit when momentum stops

4️⃣ Earnings Season Plays

Focus: Buy pre-earnings or trade post-earnings volatility.
Example: Puts on those to underperform or calls on Growth, or use a spread to trade the volatility

5️⃣ Dividend Growth Stocks

Focus: Consistent dividend payers for steady returns.
Example: Consistent income from reliable dividend names like $KO or $JNJ

6️⃣ Value vs. Growth

Focus:
	• Value: Undervalued gems
	• Growth: High-revenue potential.
Example: Value for stability, growth for upside!

7️⃣ Technical Analysis

Focus: Use charts, RSI, or moving averages to spot patterns.
Tip: Combine with fundamentals for better trades, looking at daily expected moves

8️⃣ Macro/Geopolitical Themes

Focus: Trade events like inflation, rate changes or wars
Example: Rising rates boost banks, lower rates boost small caps ($IWM), while conflicts may favor defense stocks.

9️⃣ IPO & SPAC Trading

Focus: Early moves on new market entrants.
Tip: Watch for volatility—set clear targets.

🔟 Master Risk Management

Focus: Position sizing, stop-loss orders, and disciplined trading plans.
Tip: Protect your capital and trade with consistency! You don't have to win every trade, just win big on the winners and cut the losers fast.

### Post #143: 2024-12-18 19:28 `[News]`

**✂️ Fed cut rates today!**

We got a rate cut.... But the dot plot? Looks like they're keeping an eye on inflation!   🦅

✂️ Rate Cut Decision ✂️

The Federal Reserve announced a 25 basis point (bps) cut to interest rates, bringing the federal funds rate to a range of 4.25% to 4.5%. This move was anticipated by markets, aligning with expectations for a continued easing of monetary policy. It's likely they will pause at the January meeting.

🦅 Dot Plot and Hawkish Tone 🦅

The dot plot, which represents individual Fed policymakers' projections for future interest rates, indicated a cautious outlook. The median 'dot' in the new 2025 Federal Reserve 'Dot Plot' moved 0.50% higher to 3.875%. Just two FOMC penciled in a rate below 3.50% against 14 in September.

The plot suggested that the Fed anticipates only two more rate cuts for 2025, which is fewer than some market participants had hoped for, showing a slower pace of future rate reductions

📈 Market Reaction 📉

Markets reacted with some volatility, as the hawkish tone from the Fed suggested that the era of rapid rate cuts might be moderating. Investors were interpreting this as the Fed signaling it might not be as dovish moving forward, possibly preparing for a scenario where inflation could stabilize above the target or if economic growth shows unexpected strength.

This cautious approach by the Federal Reserve reflects a balancing act between stimulating economic growth and controlling inflation, especially in a post-election environment where new fiscal policies might influence economic conditions.

### Post #146: 2024-12-19 01:49 `[Discuss]`

**Market Fear?**

Feeling the market jitters? 😰 

I was up around +48K today, and I ended the day -58k. Ouch.

If the bull market is to continue, a sentiment reset is most likely healthy, welcome, and necessary. 5783 (-5.04%) is the bottom of election gap. Will see how it unfolds in coming days/weeks. Drawdowns are calculated from recent S&P 500 closing high of 6090.

Fear and capitulation are hitting hard, but here's a little market wisdom: this could be the capitulation cycle at play. 

When everyone's selling in panic, prices drop to levels that seem irrational. But, that's often the moment before recovery begins! Think of it as the market's deep breath before the next rally. 

Here's how it goes:

1. Denial - Prices dip, but we're all in denial, thinking it'll bounce back

2. Fear - As prices keep falling, fear sets in, and selling starts.

3. Panic - Now it's a free fall with everyone rushing to sell.

4. Capitulation - The last of the sellers give up, prices hit rock bottom.

5. Despair - The market feels hopeless, but savvy investors start seeing value.

6. Rebound - Slowly, the market finds its footing and begins to climb.

So, when you see those red candles, remember, it might just be the cycle getting ready to turn. Keep your eyes open for the next upturn!

*Link: https://afterhour.com/ThetaMaestro/rnv/market-fear*

### Post #151: 2024-12-19 21:39 `[Discuss]`

**🧙‍♀️ Triple Witching Alert 🧙‍♀️**

Tomorrow, get ready for some magical market movements because it's Triple Witching Day! 📅

My guess is Mr. Market keeps the stocks down to triple kill the options, and then we Santa Rally on Monday! 🎅🏼🎄

🌟 What is Triple Witching?🌟  
- It's when stock options, stock index futures, and stock index options all expire at the same time. This happens on the third Friday of March, June, September, and December! 

🚀 What Does It Mean for the Market?🚀

- Expect increased trading volume!📈 The simultaneous expiration of these contracts can cause a lot of activity as traders close, roll over, or offset their positions.

- Volatility alert! 📉 Due to all the last-minute adjustments, the market might get a bit more unpredictable, especially in the last hour of trading, known as the "witching hour". 

- Price swings might be more dramatic, so buckle up for some potential rollercoaster rides in stock prices.

🛡️ Tips for Investors🛡️

- If you're a long-term investor, these events are usually just background noise. But, it's a good day to watch the market closely if you're into trading.

- Short-term traders might find opportunities in this chaos, but remember, with high volatility comes high risk.

So, whether you're a seasoned trader or just watching from the sidelines, keep your eyes peeled for some market action!


*Link: https://afterhour.com/ThetaMaestro/rBP/triple-witching-alert*

### Post #162: 2024-12-21 21:45 `[Discuss]`

**30-Year Mortgage Rate at 7.512%**

You thought the Fed cutting rates 3 times since September would mean cheaper loans? Think again! Despite those cuts, we're looking at 30-year mortgage rates soaring past 7% from 6.08% just months ago. 

Here's why:

1️⃣ Treasury Yields

Mortgage rates are more tied to the 10-year Treasury yield than the Fed's overnight rate. When investors see inflation or a booming economy, they want higher yields, pushing mortgage rates up.

2️⃣  Inflation Expectations

Even with rate cuts, if people expect prices to keep rising, bond investors demand higher yields to offset inflation, which in turn affects mortgage rates.

3️⃣ Market Mood

The bond market often anticipates future Fed moves or economic shifts before they happen. This anticipation can lead to higher yields despite current Fed actions.

So, what does this mean for you? 

- No soft landing for real estate. Homebuyers might need to brace for high rates for a while longer

- If you're thinking of buying, consider how long you plan to stay in your home. Fixed rates might still be a good lock-in despite the increase

### Post #171: 2024-12-26 02:15 `[Discuss]`

**🚀 Richard Dennis's - 1,600 to 200M**

Richard Dennis, known as the "Prince of the Pit," turned a modest $1,600 into a legendary $200 million through disciplined trend-following. 

His Turtle Trading experiment proved that with the right rules and mindset, anyone could succeed in trading.  

Here are the main focuses of his strategy:

🔸 **TLDR; Key Points**: 

- **Trend Following**: Buy high, sell higher; ride the market wave.  
- **Risk Management**: Never risk more than 1-2% of your capital per trade.  
- **Discipline**: Stick to your system through thick and thin.  

He conducted something called the Turtle Trading Experiment to prove that trading could be taught. It involved the training of novice traders in a systematic method over two weeks. The Turtles, as these trainees were called, then managed real capital using these strategies, collectively generating over $175 million in profits, showcasing the effectiveness of disciplined, rule-based trading.

Here are the full details of what they were taught:

- 1️⃣ **Trend Following**: Dennis believed in capitalizing on major market trends. His approach was to buy when prices were rising and to sell when they were falling, ensuring he entered trades in the direction of the trend.

- 2️⃣ **Breakout Systems**: A significant part of his strategy involved entering trades when prices broke out of established ranges. For example:

  - ⏩️ **Entry**: He would enter a long position if the price moved above the highest price of the last 20 days (for a breakout system), suggesting a new uptrend.
  - ⏩️ **Exit**: Similarly, he'd exit or go short if the price fell below the lowest price in the same period, indicating a downtrend.

- 3️⃣ **Position Sizing**: Dennis emphasized the importance of risk management. His rule was to never risk more than 1% or 2% of the total trading capital on any single trade. This was calculated based on the volatility of the market, using the Average True Range (ATR).

- 4️⃣ **Stop Losses**: He used mechanical stop-loss orders to limit losses, setting them at a distance from the entry price based on market volatility.

- 5️⃣ **Pyramiding**: Rather than investing all capital at once, Dennis would add to winning positions as they moved in his favor, hence "pyramiding" his trades. However, he would only do this up to a certain point to manage risk.

- 6️⃣ **Discipline and System Adherence**: Perhaps one of the most crucial aspects was his insistence on sticking to the system regardless of emotions or external pressures. The Turtles were taught not to deviate from the strategy even when facing losing streaks, as the system was designed to win over the long term.

- 7️⃣ **Diversification**: He traded across multiple markets – commodities, currencies, and more – to spread risk.

- 8️⃣ **Long and Short**: Dennis didn't just go long; he also shorted markets, taking advantage of both rising and falling markets.

Dennis's approach was revolutionary because it suggested that successful trading could be taught and systematized, challenging the notion that trading success was purely intuitive or based on insider knowledge. His experiment with the Turtles was a testament to this philosophy.  

Edit: for those asking per @ultrahi, he has book called: The Complete Turtle Trader
https://www.amazon.com/Complete-TurtleTrader-Investors-Overnight-Millionaires-ebook/dp/B000W94CZ8/ref=tmm_kin_swatch_0?_encoding=UTF8&qid=&sr=

### Post #184: 2024-12-29 23:24 `[Discuss]`

**My Top 12 AI Prompts**

Here are 12 prompts that I use often with AI. My preferred AI is Grok but this also works with ChatGPT and others.  

-----------------   


1. **Improve your problem-solving abilities:**

   - "Your job is to solve problems. Give me a step-by-step guide to solve this problem: [insert]."

2. **Summarize complex texts:**

   - "I need to read a complicated article related to (topic). Can you help me summarise the key points and takeaways from the text?"

3. **Connect with others in your field:**

   - "Connect me with a community of learners and experts in (topic). How can I join a forum, social media group or other online community to share my knowledge and learn from others?"

4. **Use AI to write a speech:**

   - "Write a launch speech for [product/business] that highlights the values of the [company or niche], and addresses a widespread problem or mistake."

5. **Develop a social media content strategy:**

   - "Create a social media content strategy for [social media handles] for [time period] to attract [target audience]."

6. **Respond professionally to emails:**

   - "As a customer service representative, you are tasked with handling a case involving [PROBLEM]. You must write an email responding to that problem, no more than [NUMBER OF WORDS] words long, offering the customer [SOLUTION]."

7. **Text, article, or research paper analysis:**

   - "Please analyze the following text and provide: A concise summary (2–3 sentences), Key takeaways in bullet points, and any relevant references or data points to support the arguments. [Paste / Summarize Text or Add link Here]"

8. **Generate new ideas for a topic:**

   - "I need fresh ideas for [TOPIC]. Describe at least 5 innovative and practical ideas that can be implemented."

9. **Become my tutor and teach me:**

   - "Act as a personal tutor to help me learn [Skill or Topic]. Create a [Timeframe] study plan with weekly goals, practice exercises, and quizzes. Include recommended resources like articles or videos. Ensure the difficulty increases progressively."

10. **Transform my content:**

    - "Rewrite the following text in a [Desired Tone or Style]. Then provide a short summary for [Platform or Context]. [Paste Content Here]"

11. **Personal Finance Brainstorm:**

    - "Act as a general financial advisor. I need help with [Financial Goal]. Suggest possible approaches, budgeting tips, and common pitfalls to avoid."

12. **Personalize my event or trip:**

    - "Plan a [Type of Event or Trip] for [Number of People] in [Location] within a [Budget Range]. Include lodging options, daily activities, and must-see attractions."

13. **BONUS: The 20/80 Rule** per @DapperDan

    - "I want to learn about [topic]. Identify and share the most important 20% of learning from this topic that will help me understand 80% of it."

What other prompts do you use?

*Link: https://afterhour.com/ThetaMaestro/tGy/my-top-12-ai-prompts*

### Post #186: 2025-01-01 21:10 `[News]`

**If a Failed Santa Rally... Then What?**

In 1999, a significant Santa Claus rally failure was followed by a substantial market downturn over the next few years, culminating in the dot-com bubble burst.

In 2007, another notable year without a Santa Claus rally preceded the 2008 financial crisis.

**So what does this mean for 2025?**

The first two trading days of January are an integral part of the Santa Claus rally period, tying it to broader January market patterns, including the "January Barometer,"

The January Barometer suggests that January's market performance often sets the tone for the rest of the year. Combined with the Santa Claus rally and the market's performance in the first five trading days of January—this is called the "January Trifecta"—these indicators can help discern patterns in the market. 

📉 **So What Does This Mean for January?**
- Historically, a flat or negative Santa Rally leads to January gains only 40% of the time
- The first quarter tends to be flat on average
- The full year has seen an average median gain of 3% following a lackluster Santa Rally

💡 **Takeaway:**
- Diversify your investments to mitigate risks.
- Stay informed on economic news and policy changes

"I think the S&P 500 can get to 7,000 by the end of the year... But it's not going to be a straight line, and I would think in the next few weeks things are going to be somewhat bumpy".

 - 1995-1999

- **Baby Boomers Retiring:** They are sitting on 75 trillion and due to RMDs it will fuel spending in travel, healthcare, and more. It's not just about retiring; it's about spending their nest eggs on services, which means more jobs! 

- **Productivity Boost:** As Boomers step back, Millennials and Gen Z are stepping up. New tech and ways of working could lead to productivity gains despite the demographic shift!

### Post #197: 2025-01-07 04:30 `[News]`

**$NVDA CES 2025 - Recap**

Here is a recap of the top items I noted during the $NVDA Keynote at CES 2025

**Summarized Recap**

1. Physical AI World Foundation Models (Cosmos): Openly available for developing robotics and autonomous vehicles.
2. Agentic AI: Test-time scaling for real-time reasoning and decision-making.
3. Humanoid Robots: Pioneering robotics development powered by NVIDIA's hardware and software ecosystems.
4. Self-Driving Cars: Partnerships with Toyota and others to power next-generation autonomous vehicles.
5. NVIDIA is not just building tools—it’s laying the foundation for an AI-powered future that spans industries and transforms how we live and work

----------------

**Detailed Recap**

 -  **Next AI Phase is physical** - Nvidia Cosmos platform is the world foundational model for robotics on CUDA. This will train multi modality foundational models. Open licensed world platform. This is lllama 3 for physical AI.

 -  **Unveiled the RTX 5070** - a very affordably priced at $549 and delivering RTX 4090-level performance (world's current fastest GPU). This might be the most disruptive GPU release yet!

 - **Introduced Grace Blackwell NVLink 72** - featuring 72 Blackwell GPUs, 2,592 Grace CPU cores, and 130 trillion transistors. This system, delivering 4x performance per watt, will be key for powering Agentic AIs that leverage test-time scaling for real-time reasoning and decision-making.

 - **Agentic AIs** - Will use test-time scaling to "think" through complex problems in steps, adapting their reasoning much like humans. Blackwell’s efficiency and compute density position it as the backbone of this new era, where AI will handle advanced planning, problem-solving, and automation.

 - **Llama Nemotron Language Foundation Models** in 3 tiers - These models dominate benchmarks, ranking #1 on MTEB, ChatRAG, and RewardBench, demonstrating their superiority across performance, retrieval, and reward optimization tasks. NVIDIA's Llama models redefine AI deployment from edge to enterprise-scale environments.

 - **Expanding into the EV secto** - Partnering with Toyota to power their next-generation cars with NVIDIA supercomputers and DriveOS. These vehicles will feature automated driving capabilities, leveraging NVIDIA’s cloud-to-car strategy for AI development and deployment. Toyota’s EVs will use NVIDIA’s Drive AGX Orin for real-time AI processing, combined with NVIDIA DGX for training and Omniverse for simulation. This partnership positions NVIDIA as a key player in the EV and autonomous vehicle market, a space currently dominated by Tesla. With NVIDIA’s technology at its core, Toyota’s new EVs could intensify competition in the sector and accelerate the adoption of safe, autonomous driving systems.

 - **NVIDIA DIGITS** - a deep learning GPU training system, describing it as "a cloud computing platform that sits on your desk." Powered by the compact yet powerful GB110 chip, DIGITS delivers enterprise-grade AI capabilities in a highly efficient package. DIGITS will be available around May 2025, enabling researchers and developers to bring cutting-edge AI capabilities to their desktops.


$NVDA is changing the world as we know it... Buy buy buy!!

### Post #203: 2025-01-08 21:39 `[Discuss]`

**FOMC Dec 2024 - Minutes**

Here are the main points from the Federal Open Market Committee (FOMC) minutes:

**RATE POLICY:**

 - A 25bps rate cut was broadly supported, with the majority favoring a cautious approach to further easing.
 - Many participants suggested that a variety of factors underlined the need for a careful approach to monetary policy decisions over coming
quarters
 - Some participants noted it might be prudent to pause rate cuts if inflation readings remain above target or economic momentum persists.
 - A few officials highlighted potential scenarios to accelerate cuts if inflation trends lower or labor market softens more than expected.
 - Many emphasized the importance of carefully assessing the neutral rate and moving gradually to avoid policy missteps.

**RISK OUTLOOK:**

 - Inflation risks remain balanced, though higher-than-expected recent readings warrant close monitoring.
 - Labor market risks were deemed manageable, with no rapid deterioration expected.

**ECONOMIC CONTEXT:**

 - Inflation progress has slowed but remains on a downward path; core PCE inflation was noted at 2.8% in October.
 - Labor market conditions have eased slightly, but unemployment remains low at 4.2%.
 - Participants expect solid GDP growth to continue, though some noted financial strains for lower-income households.

Minutes ➡️ https://www.federalreserve.gov/newsevents/pressreleases/monetary20250108a.htm

*Link: https://afterhour.com/ThetaMaestro/wPX/fomc-dec-2024-minutes*

### Post #205: 2025-01-09 16:32 — `$RXRX` `[News]`

**$RXRX is ready to 🚀**

$RXRX has advanced two investigational oncology drugs into clinical trials:

1. **REC-3565** - A potential best-in-class MALT1 inhibitor, designed to reduce the risk of hyperbilirubinemia, a common side effect of other MALT1 inhibitors. The UK Medicines and Healthcare Products Regulatory Agency (MHRA) has cleared a Phase 1 trial for this drug, which targets B-cell malignancies. The addressable market could include approximately 41,000 patients in the US and EU5 with relapsed/refractory chronic lymphocytic leukemia and B-cell lymphomas.

2. **REC-4539** - A potential best-in-class, reversible, and CNS-penetrant LSD1 inhibitor for small-cell lung cancer (SCLC) and other potential indications. The FDA has cleared an IND application for a Phase 1/2 trial, focusing initially on SCLC, with a total addressable population in the US+EU5 for extensive stage SCLC estimated at around 45,000 patients annually. This drug is unique as it can target both peripheral disease and brain metastases, common in SCLC.

Both advances are examples of how Recursion's AI-powered platform, the Recursion OS, enables the identification and optimization of molecules with unique properties, aiming to meet high unmet medical needs in oncology.

https://www.biopharmatrend.com/post/1095-recursion-advances-two-investigational-oncology-drugs-to-clinical-trials/

### Post #243: 2025-01-27 18:04 `[Discuss]`

**Lesson In Options - $SOFI**

To my fellow $SOFI bagholders, respectfully please learn from this lesson:

**STOP DOING WEEKLY CALLS**

The market can be unreasonable, and this is why I pay more for TIME. Chances are my calls will still print... I really like $SOFI long-term and I'm not even slightly worried about this short term selloff. They had a triple beat and raised guidance!

Here's why you do options that are 3-6 months:

- **More Breathing Room:** Longer options give your stock picks more time to move in your favor. Weekly options can expire worthless if the timing's off by just a few days.

- **Slower Time Decay:** Options lose value over time, but this decay is much less severe for 3-6 month calls, giving you a bigger window before the value drops significantly.

- **Volatility Buffer:** Short-term options are hit harder by volatility spikes; longer terms are generally less affected, offering more stability.

- **Less Hands-On:** With longer expirations, you don't need to watch the markets like a hawk, potentially saving on trading costs and stress.

- **Peace of Mind:** Knowing you have months, not days, can make trading less like a race against time.

While longer calls cost more upfront, they can provide a safer strategy for those looking to avoid the high-risk game of timing the market

*Link: https://afterhour.com/ThetaMaestro/z0S/lesson-in-options-dollarsofi*

### Post #265: 2025-02-15 20:48 — `$TSLA` `[Discuss]`

**$UBER vs. $TSLA in AVs**

"$UBER is going to zero when the cybercab comes out!" is what the Tesla fanboys are shouting. This narrative is ignorant - Uber is set to crush it in the autonomous vehicle (AV) game compared to Tesla. 

I put my money where my mouth is and bought **$123,000** in options that they were wrong, and it's paid off! Oh, and I own Tesla's... so I'm not a hater.

Here's why they're wrong:

First off, Uber’s network is massive. The Uber app has been downloaded over 1.2 **BILLION** times. They do 33 million rides per day, and recently had a milestone of 1 million concurrent rides. They also have insane tech and logistics which help them get drivers where needed, and rides completed.

**AV Owners** - If you’ve got a Tesla with FSD that has become an AV in the future, are you going to plug it into the Tesla app where you may get 2 rides for the evening? No, you’d be way better off plugging it into Uber’s network where you can get 10-15 rides. I can also buy 10 cybercabs and plug them into Ubers network - why do I need Tesla? More saturation, more rides, more money—it’s that simple.

**Ride Hailers / AV Future** - But here’s where Uber really flexes: they’re not just banking on Tesla’s tech. They’re teaming up with big AV players like WeRide and Waymo, too. So, when you open the Uber app, you’ll be able to pick a driverless ride if you’re feeling futuristic, or stick with a human driver if you’re not quite ready to let go of the wheel. Options, baby! And because Uber’s got such a huge network, wait times are going to be way shorter than anything Tesla could dream up on its own.

**Operational Expenses** – Let’s be real: if Tesla decides to own and manage its own Cybercab fleets, the costs are going to be astronomical. We’re talking massive capital expenditures—building the vehicles, setting up storage facilities, installing charging stations, and somehow achieving enough market saturation to even dream of challenging Uber’s dominance. It’s a logistical nightmare. On the flip side, if Tesla opts to sell Cybercabs to folks like me, who might snap up a few, why on earth would I limit myself to Tesla’s tiny app to find riders? I’d list them on Uber’s platform in a heartbeat, where the sheer volume of users—1.2 billion downloads strong—means more rides, more money, and less downtime. It’s a no-brainer.

Now, let’s talk about Uber’s secret weapon: their biggest expense is paying drivers. If they can phase out human drivers and go all-in on AVs, it’s a game-changer. Riders save money with cheaper fares, and Uber’s profits soar as they cut their largest cost. It’s a win-win that Tesla simply can’t match, no matter how cool their Cybercabs look.

Oh, and let’s not forget—Uber’s not just a ride company anymore. They’re raking in cash from stuff like Uber Eats, Uber One, and even freight logistics. Tesla? They’re stuck selling cars and hoping their robotaxi dreams pan out. Uber’s already got the revenue streams to keep the party going, no matter what. 

So, yeah, if I’m betting on who’s going to own the AV future, my money’s on Uber, hands down.

### Post #268: 2025-02-16 17:35 — `$UBER` `[Discuss]`

**My Options  Trading Plan**

Hello... options swing trader here! 

My goal is to win 50% of my trades, but when I do win... It's big!

Like, bookmark and share as this is my trading plan 👇👇👇

**Objective**
The objective of this trading plan is to execute disciplined, risk-managed options trades to achieve consistent returns while minimizing losses. All trades will adhere strictly to the rules and strategies outlined below, with **no exceptions**.

---

**Portfolio Management Rules**
- **Trade Size Limits**  
  - Individual stock trades must not exceed **25% of the portfolio**.  
  - **Exception**: Index funds or ETFs may exceed this limit, but must still be risk-assessed.

- **Expiration Diversification**  
  - **80%** of trades must have expiration dates **greater than 1 month**.  
  - **20%** of trades may be weeklies, but only if a clear opportunity presents itself.

- **Prohibited Expirations**  
  - No trades with **0DTE to 7DTE** (0 to 7 days to expiration), as these are considered high-risk gambling.

- **Hedging High-Risk Trades**  
  - For trades with a **50/50 risk profile**, purchase **ITM short-term puts** as a hedge.  
  - Hedge cost must not exceed **20% of the call value** (target 5-7% if no catalyst).  
  - Purpose of puts: Gain short-term value; if they expire worthless, the long position should offset losses.

- **Strike Price Selection Based on Expiration**  
  - For **shorter-dated calls (30 days)**: Purchase **ITM or ATM** options (avoid deep ITM).  
  - For **longer-dated calls (6+ months)**: Purchase **OTM LEAPS** with strikes approximately **10% above current price** to maximize returns.

- **Risk Analysis Tools**  
  - Use an **options calculator** to evaluate:  
    - **Theta decay** (time decay impact).  
    - **Delta** (sensitivity to stock price movement).  

---

**Trading Strategy**

1. **Entry Criteria**
To enter a trade, the following conditions must be met:  
- **Catalyst Requirement**  
  - A clear catalyst must be present, such as:  
    - Earnings reports.  
    - Stock splits.  
    - Macroeconomic events (e.g., CPI data, interest rate changes).  
    - Geopolitical developments.  
    - Strong stock trends supported by fundamental analysis.  
    - Excessively Oversold for a dead-cat bounce

- **Strike Price and Expiration Selection**  
  - For **shorter-dated calls (30 days)**: Buy **ITM or ATM** options.  
  - For **longer-dated calls (6+ months)**: Buy **OTM LEAPS** with strikes ~10% above current price.  

- **Stock Selection**  
  - Focus on **lower-volatility stocks** that are in S&P500 or ETFs (e.g., SPY, QQQ) to minimize risk
  - Ensure **decent trading volume** to guarantee liquidity for exiting trades.

- **Delta Target**  
  - Aim for a **Delta** of approximately **0.70** (may not always be achievable, but prioritize higher Delta for ITM/ATM options).  

- **Timing of Purchase**  
  - For **ATM options**, ensure at least **3 to 4 weeks until expiration**.  
  - Buy at the **low of the day or week** to optimize entry price.

2. **Exit Criteria**
The following rules govern when to sell options:  
  
- **Theta Decay Monitoring**  
  - Sell if **theta decay** begins eroding profits, especially as expiration nears.  
  - If the stock is expected to continue rising (based on catalysts), consider rebuying with a **longer expiration**.  

- **Profit Targets and Timeframes**  

  - **Goal is 100% profit** (e.g. bought option for $2, sell for $4)
  - Cut the Losers quickly, if momentum turns negative
  - Let the Winners run until momentum stop
 
- **Re-Entry Rules**  
  - If re-entering a position, **extend the expiration date** to ensure sufficient time.  
  - Do not re-enter the same position unless it still has **30+ days until expiration**.

---

**Example Trade**

**Scenario: Buying Call Options on $UBER**

- **Current Stock Price**: $79.42  
- **Position**: Buy  calls with a **06/20/2025 expiration** and a **$85 strike**.  
- **COST**: The current contract price is  **$5.00** per contract
- **EXIT**: My goal would be to exit before May as that's when Theta accelerates, and at a price of  **$10.00** per contract or greater (100% return)



*Link: https://afterhour.com/ThetaMaestro/DJS/my-options-trading-plan*

### Post #296: 2025-03-10 17:36 `[Discuss]`

**The Real Story Here - Bond Yields**

This is my theory - The market isn’t down because of selloff, it’s about liquidity. The bond market dictates credit issuance, which fuels the stock and crypto markets. When bond yields spike, debt markets tighten, credit dries up, and investors are forced to liquidate.

Trump has made comments about short-term pain. I think he's using a controlled selloff (tariffs, etc) to lower Bond Yields.

- **Theory**: Trump wants to lower bond yields to reduce the cost of borrowing for the U.S. government, given the need to refinance approximately $7 trillion in debt maturing soon.
- **Strategy**: Encourage a controlled market selloff (e.g., stocks, crypto) to create uncertainty, prompting investors to flock to the safety of U.S. Treasury bonds.
- **Mechanism**: Increased demand for Treasuries would raise bond prices, lowering yields—like pushing the 10-year Treasury rate from its current 4.2% closer to 3.5% or below.
- **Objective**: Secure cheaper refinancing for the $7 trillion in debt issued at lower rates years ago, saving billions in interest as yields have risen since mid-2024.￼
- **Follow-Up**: Once yields drop and refinancing is locked in, ease off the selloff pressure by softening aggressive policy rhetoric (e.g., tariffs, spending) and pivot to pro-growth measures like tax cuts, deregulation, and energy policy.
- **Context**: Bond yields have already dipped since January 2025 amid tariff fears and economic uncertainty, aligning with Trump’s stated goal (via Treasury pick Scott Bessent) of engineering lower long-term rates.
- **Risks**: If the selloff deepens uncontrollably, it could backfire—spiking yields higher if investors panic over debt sustainability or lose faith in Trump’s economic management, thus causing a recession.

*Link: https://afterhour.com/ThetaMaestro/Hk5/the-real-story-here-bond-yield*

💳 Consumer Behavior Adjustments – Higher interest rates and economic conditions have slowed demand, reducing price pressures in key categories.

### Post #306: 2025-03-19 19:48 `[News]`

**JPOW Statement Summary**

The big news was that Fed Chairman Powell ignored the University of Michigan's data on what people think inflation will be, saying that opinions can shift. He also said the Fed is prepared to act. This is a different tune from what he said back in January. LOW IS IN

Most important Powell statement at the presser:

- Confirms that Fed's baseline assumption is tariffs' effect on prices is one-time, and if that appears to hold true again, the one-time rise won't change the Fed's rate stance. DOVISH.

Second most  important Powell statement (paraphrased):

- Yes, we saw the UofM survey of inflation expectations. That kind of survey result has become worthless. Less risk nowadays that such "expectations" will become a self fulfilling prophecy.

Summary of Powell's speech from The Fed:

- The economy remains strong. Labor market conditions are solid. Inflation is still somewhat high.  
- The Fed has made a technical decision to slow the pace of balance sheet reduction.  
- Surveys show increasing economic uncertainty.  
- PCE prices are likely to rise 2.5% in December.  
- Recent inflation expectations have increased, with tariffs as a driving factor.  
- Long-term inflation expectations remain consistent with the 2% target.

### Post #323: 2025-04-10 05:00 `[Discuss]`

**🚨 Chamath Explains Hidden Truth of Tariffs**

Chamath Palihapitiya’s viral interview with Andrew Schulz reveals the hidden truth behind Trump’s $750B tariff strategy! 🇺🇸💰

- Trump’s tariffs aren’t just a trade war with China—they’re a massive wealth redistribution plan to rebuild American manufacturing. Since 2000, the U.S. has lost 5M+ manufacturing jobs, hollowing out the middle class.
- Palihapitiya, a billionaire investor and former Facebook exec, explains: Tariffs (10-60%) and tax breaks incentivize companies to bring production back to the U.S., potentially reviving communities, creating jobs, and boosting national security.
- The plan could generate $750B annually, offsetting middle-class taxes while reducing reliance on foreign goods (like PPE, semiconductors, and energy).
- It’s a global trade shift—Trump’s creating an “economic NATO,” using tariffs as leverage to align nations with U.S. interests.
- For decades, unbalanced trade with China (25%+ tariffs on U.S. goods vs. 3% on Chinese goods) benefited corporations, not citizens, by cutting labor costs and avoiding regulations.
- The pandemic and global conflicts exposed U.S. vulnerabilities: reliance on foreign nations for critical goods like PPE, energy, and semiconductors threatens national security.
- Risks include inflation, WTO violations, and supply chain disruptions, but the long-term goal is a manufacturing renaissance and a stronger, more independent America.

What do you think? 🧐 

Full video: https://youtu.be/EmDt_zAlL8E

### Post #347: 2025-05-16 00:30 — `$MSTR` `[Yolo]`

**$MTPLF: The Bitcoin Treasury**


This is everything you need to know about MetaPlanet ($MTPLF)... And why I'm betting on them with 33,000 shares. Goal is 100,000 shares by end of May 🫦

**Who is MetaPlanet? ⤵️**

MetaPlanet is a bold player in the Bitcoin treasury space, transitioning from its roots in hospitality to a crypto-focused powerhouse. Originally a Japanese hotel and real estate company, Metaplanet pivoted in 2024 to embrace Bitcoin as a core treasury asset, aiming to hedge against inflation and fiat devaluation. They hold 6,796 BTC valued at $700M—a move that’s turned heads in the crypto world. 

They recently launched on the OTC markets (not available on RH, but you can buy on Merrill, Fidelity, and Schwab). We're truly that early to the US markets - even though they have a year of proven track record as a Bitcoin Treasury company.

**But how do they acquire Bitcoin is what really makes them special ⤵️**

They leverage the Japanese Yen carry trade, borrowing yen at low interest rates to fund BTC purchases, capitalizing on yen depreciation to amplify returns. They also sell cash-secured put options, generating premiums while positioning to buy Bitcoin at lower prices if the options are exercised. Additionally, Metaplanet raises cash through zero-coupon bonds—like a ¥2B issuance in March 2025—and stock acquisition rights, such as their 11th series in 2024, channeling proceeds directly into Bitcoin investments. This multi-pronged strategy fuels their aggressive Bitcoin accumulation.

**Why does anyone pay a premium for a Bitcoin Treasury company, instead of just buying Bitcoin directly? ⤵️**

It has to do with mNAV, or market Net Asset Value, which is a ratio that compares a company's market capitalization to the net asset value of its holdings. It indicates how much the market is willing to pay for each dollar of a company's underlying assets, with a higher mNAV suggesting greater investor confidence or speculative premium. In short, now much Bitcoin can they acquire per share they issue. MetaPlanet currently yields 2.7x more Bitcoin per share than buying Bitcoin directly. It's basically a leveraged option without time decay or expiration!

**But how do they compare to other Bitcoin Treasury companies? ⤵️**

 $MTPLF trades at an mNAV of 2.7 (has even been higher) with a $1.9B market cap, showing investors are paying a premium for its Bitcoin holdings. $MSTR, the largest Bitcoin holder with 528,185 BTC, often trades at an mNAV around 1.9, while $CEP (Cathedra Bitcoin) typically hovers lower, around 1.8–2.0, based on recent data from bitcointreasuries.net. $MTPLF’s premium reflects its growing hype as Asia’s Bitcoin treasury pioneer!

**Want to dive deeper into $MTPLF? Check out these resources: ⤵️**

- 🎥 CEO Interview with Simon Gerovich: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hZNa6aPzfig&ab_channel=BitcoinMagazine

### Post #360: 2025-05-21 14:53 `[Discuss]`

**Short Squeeze out of this (Meta)Planet! 🪐**

There is a very real  chance that Metaplanet can’t trade for the rest of the week on the Tokyo Stock Exchange due to limit-up halts. This is the short squeeze that GME and MSTR dreamed of.

Here's what is going on with $MTPLF and the situation with Japan's stock market rules:

🚨 Japan’s stock market has strict daily price limits—stocks like Metaplanet (MTPLF) can’t trade if they surge too fast! 📈 MTPLF, Japan’s *most shorted stock*, is in a massive short squeeze. Most of the available share float is in Japan, but Hedge funds can’t cover as the US market drives prices higher, triggering Tokyo Stock Exchange halt

-----

- **Japan’s stock market limits**: The Tokyo Stock Exchange (TSE) enforces daily price limits to prevent extreme volatility. For example, if a stock hits its upper limit (e.g., a 150 yen increase for a 1,000 yen closing price, per Japan Exchange Group rules), trading can be halted if certain conditions are met, such as no shares being traded at the limit price or high remaining bids/offers.

- **Metaplanet as the most shorted stock**: MetaPlanet CEO confirmed that Metaplanet is Japan’s most shorted stock, with heavy short interest from hedge funds betting against its Bitcoin-driven rally.

- **US market lifting the price**:  The US market under $MTPLF pushes the price higher, creating pressure on short sellers. Since the TSE halts trading when limits are hit, short sellers struggle to cover their positions, exacerbating the squeeze.

This is actually insane.


*Link: https://afterhour.com/ThetaMaestro/Or7/short-squeeze-out-of-this-meta*

2. Price Ticks: Adjusted in preset increments depending on the price range: e.g., 5 JPY increments for 1,000 - 3,000 JPY range

3. Escalating Steps: If no orders are matched, the indicative price is revised upward/downward at regular intervals.

4. Execution: The auction clears only once sufficient matching orders are available.

### Post #384: 2025-06-08 13:37 `[Discuss]`

**Some Advice**

I'm seeing a lot of weird content on here by some really young people.

Not that my opinion is of any value, but here are some things that I've learned over the years:

1. **Earn followers by adding value**. Teach, educate and help people learn to make money. Tearing others down, saying your more valuable than others on a list, and asking when you'll be on lists... Tells me you don't add enough value yet to be worthy of those lists.

2. **Your ego should follow your port size**. I don't want this to sound mean and my port isn't massive, but this app was designed with the "port transparency" concept so I feel it's worth mentioning. Having less than $50,000 and acting like you own the app is really just silly. It's not that you can't or don't add value - I learn from everyone and you should absolutely teach me and others. And it's not that having money makes you more valuable either, but a small port size means you're likely still a young trader and are in the learning phase. Imagine a rookie chef just out of culinary school, tells the seasoned head chef to use less salt in the sauce, unaware that the chef’s decades of experience have perfected the recipe’s balance. I see a lot of young chefs telling people to add salt and losing people money.

3. **People who actually have money, really don't talk about it**. Period. I know probably 20 people with 10+ million IRL and they don't talk about it ever. It's usually a humility thing, but also their success speaks for itself and they want privacy about it. It's usually the guys posting pictures of their lambos that have the repo guy right behind them. And to prove my point, there are plenty of whales on here who have generational wealth - do you notice a difference in how they speak on here compared to some of the other guys?

### Post #392: 2025-06-14 18:30 — `$MSTR` `[Yolo]`

**MetaPlanet to $105**

🚀 When MetaPlanet ($MTPLF) hits $105, people will say it’s luck that I started buying at $3.80 - but the signs were clear! I'm out here sounding the trumpet. 🎺

Now trading at ~$11.12/share, let’s dive into why $MTPLF is poised for explosive growth, backed by cold, hard math👇

MetaPlanet’s edge lies in its Bitcoin Treasury model, which thrives on raising capital quickly, diluting shares strategically, and stacking Bitcoin faster than competitors. 

People ask why you should buy this over $CEP or even $MSTR. A key metric here is mNAV (market Net Asset Value) being higher than both — high mNAV is not a flaw, it’s a feature! This means $MTPLF secures more Bitcoin per share dilution, positioning them to scale their Bitcoin holdings rapidly. You want this when you have a company trying to acquire 1% of all the Bitcoin ever mined.

Competitors growth will be constrained by their ability to multiply Bitcoin stacks compared to $MTPLF ~ both due to their existing size (too large already... less ability to even 2x their shareprice) or if newer, a proven plan to execute BTC acquisition. 

MetaPlanet has been doing this for a year, and does it faster than any other company. They've gone from 0 BTC to 8888 BTC in less than a year. Their acquisition rate is increasing, and I estimate they'll have 100,000 BTC by year-end. That's over 10x their current holdings, and they have outlined guidance and a plan on how they'll accomplish this.

For $CEP to go from 42,000 BTC to 100,000 BTC means it will only 2x their Bitcoin (shareprice reflects future holdings). They are also newer to the game, and have a lot to prove that they can even do this in 6 months.

Here’s the math breakdown for $MTPLF over the next 6-12 months:

- **Bitcoin price**: Let’s assume a conservative 2x increase, in line with market trends.
- **mNAV**: Could adjust to ~0.5x (down to a multiple of ~3), reflecting market dynamics.
- **BTC/share**: With MetaPlanet’s aggressive capital raises, this could soar to 10x as they dilute and buy Bitcoin faster than peers.

Put it together: 

- 2 (Bitcoin price) x 0.5 (mNAV) x 10 (BTC/share) = 10x share price, or ~$105/share. 

This isn’t a wild guess—it’s a projection based on their current guidance and stated 555 Plan. They have given themselves 2 years to complete this, but my guess is they'll complete it in 6-8 months. $MTPLF’s structure and execution make it a standout, while $CEP’s slower Bitcoin accumulation limits its upside.

$MTPLF isn’t luck—it’s math at work. Don't say there weren't signs, or that I didn't warn you.

### Post #403: 2025-06-21 19:21 — `$MTPLF, $STRF` `[Discuss]`

**MetaPlanet Supercharging to 210K BTC**

How will MetaPlanet ($MTPLF, 3350.T) supercharge their Bitcoin strategy? 🚀

Simple; they will issue a fixed-yield product like Strategy has done with $STRF. 

Metaplanet doesn’t currently offer preferred stock, but I believe their September 2025 meeting will call for a vote to launch them. 

On June 14th, Shinpei Okuno (their Head of Capital Strategy and IR) said:

- “We have been consistently studying Strategy. Their use of preferred shares is groundbreaking, innovative, and impressive.”

And why would this supercharge their strategy? 🤔 Because Japan is insanely yield-starved... Let me explain.

Japan’s inflation is running hot at 3.6% (up from the usual 2-3%), and traditional yields— like savings accounts at 0.4% or bonds at 1-2% — are leaving investors starving for returns. 

So now let's imagine MetaPlanet rolls out a preferred offering with 5% yields— which you can't find hardly anywhere in Japan — it would see a ton of interest and gain popularity.

Now imagine they offer 10% — which is literally unheard of — it could spark a frenzy and demand would go parabolic! 🤯 

More capital pouring in means more Bitcoin buys, more put options sold, and more revenue. In my view, a competitive preferred share offering could propel Metaplanet to stack 210,000 BTC in under a year! 🚀

With 10,000 BTC (~$1.07B) already in their treasury and a stock up 2500% in a year, Metaplanet’s leading Asia’s Bitcoin charge. 🔥 And preferred shares will make them unstoppable.

🪐**MetaPlanet ($MTPLF) - Notice of Additional Capital Contribution to U.S. Subsidiary**

**TLDR** - They already completed original 250M capital movement to US Subsidary, and now are looking to do another 5 Billion.  In short, they will use their 555 Plan to raise capital by diluting shares (as previously announced), and then move up to 5B from those proceeds to the US to buy the Bitcoin.
