### Post #429: 2025-07-01 15:28 — `$MSTR` `[Discuss]`

**MetaPlanet & Preferred Shares**

Will Metaplanet ($MTPLF) offer preferred shares, and why would this even matter?

Simon (CEO), Dylan (Dir. of Bitcoin) and Shinpei (Head of IR) have been dropping tons of hints on X about Metaplanet potentially issuing Bitcoin-backed preferred shares. 

💥 Here’s why I think this is super bullish for MetaPlanet:

1: What Are Preferred Shares? Preferred shares are a type of stock that pay fixed dividends, giving holders priority over common shareholders for payouts (but no voting rights). Unlike common shares, which rely on stock price growth, preferred shares act like a hybrid of stocks and bonds, offering steady income. This makes them a magnet for yield-hungry investors, especially in Japan’s low-rate market! If you live on a fixed income and rely on dividend payments, having a preferred share offering is much more attractive than holding a volatile stock with no monthly or quarterly payment.

2: How Did Preferred Shares Help Strategy ($MSTR)? $MSTR used convertible debt and preferred shares to raise billions for Bitcoin buys without heavily diluting common shareholders. In short, it attracts diverse investor segments, which provides more cash, to then go and buy more Bitcoin.￼

3: Metaplanet’s killing it as “Asia’s MSTR,” stacking 13,350 BTC already! Shinpei hinted at a fixed-income preferred share product dropping soon. This could unlock massive capital for more Bitcoin buys without diluting us common shareholders. Using share dilution to buy Bitcoin is a part of Bitcoin Treasury companies, but this offers another tool with less dilution, which means 🚀 stock price!

4: MetaPlanet has been flexing with 74.9B JPY ($0.51B) raised through 54M shares for their “555 Million Plan.” The Japanese market is very yield starved, with average yields around 0.5% - 2%. Therefore offering Preferred shares at 4-5% could see parabolic inflows.

5: Simon pointed out Metaplanet’s the most shorted stock in Japan (25%+ short interest)! Preferred shares could spark a massive short squeeze, especially in less liquid markets. Betting against Metaplanet is like betting against Bitcoin—good luck with that! 😎

6: As far as timeline, I expect a vote at the September shareholder meeting with a resounding approval, and then with final approvals and launch by late 2025.

In summary, as Dyaln says in the post below:

"These instruments are built to capitalize on the fiat system’s inherent design: 21,000,000 BTC versus an unlimited and ever-expanding supply of currency units."

### Post #446: 2025-07-26 06:45 `[Yolo]`

**Power of Large Numbers: Why Time Favors the Patient**

If you flip a coin just 10 times, you might get 7 heads or even 8 tails — randomness dominates. But flip it 200 times, and you’ll likely see something close to 100 heads and 100 tails. That’s the law of large numbers: the more trials, the closer you get to the true probability — in this case, 50/50.

The same principle applies to long-term investing.

Take MetaPlanet ($MTPLF). In the short term — days or even months — price action can seem random, driven by emotion, news cycles, and liquidity. But over years, fundamentals and strategy take over. MetaPlanet’s Bitcoin-centric treasury strategy is a long-term thesis. Over time, as Bitcoin adoption rises and global monetary trends evolve, the odds of $MTPLF outperforming increase dramatically.

So don’t judge your flips too soon.... the real edge shows up with time!


*Link: https://afterhour.com/ThetaMaestro/US0/power-of-large-numbers-why-tim*

### Post #454: 2025-08-01 16:18 — `$MSTR` `[Dd]`

**Everything about MetaPlanet Preferred Shares**

**What Happened:**

- Metaplanet Files ¥555B ($3.69B) Shelf Registration for Two Classes of Perpetual Preferred Stock
- This is extremely bullish, and entirely NOT priced in

**What Are Preferred Shares:**

- Preferred shares are a class of stock that typically offer fixed dividends and priority over common shares in the event of liquidation, but usually don’t carry voting rights. 

- For a company like $MTPLF, issuing preferred shares can attract investment without diluting common share value, since preferred shareholders are less focused on stock price growth. The target audience is those needing fixed income, in particular institutional investment funds.

- This is copying the master play by $MSTR with their STRK, STRF and STRC preferred shares. But there are tons of preferred offering in US markets, and only 2-3 in Japan. Japan is yield starved... So this is huge and there will be massive demand!

**What Types will MetaPlanet offer:**

- Class A: Senior, Fixed, Cumulative, ≤6% Dividend
- Class B: Convertible, Cumulative, ≤6% Dividend

**How Much:**

- 555 Billion Yen (3.7 Billion USD)
- This allows them to buy roughly 33,000 BTC

**When**

- A vote will be held at Metaplanet’s Upcoming Extraordinary General Meeting of Shareholders to approve amendments to the Articles of Incorporation, laying the groundwork for the potential issuance of two classes of perpetual preferred shares.
- My estimation is likely EOY or Q1

**Mission**

- Lead a Renaissance in Japan’s Fixed Income Capital Markets

### Post #470: 2025-08-22 16:11 `[News]`

**This is a big deal**

The Fed used to say:

- It wanted inflation to average 2% over time (so if it ran below 2% for a while, it would allow above 2% later to balance out).
- It judged the job market by looking at shortfalls from maximum employment (basically focusing only when employment was too low).

By removing this language, Powell is signaling:

- The Fed will now aim for 2% inflation as a straightforward target, not an average.
- They don’t need to “make up” for past inflation misses
- It will look at employment more broadly, not just when it’s weak.

**MetaPlanet - Recent Disclosures**

You can read the ChatGPT summary below. But here's what it means for MetaPlanet via two disclosures announced last night.

$MTPLF will pause MSW (share dilution) for the next 18 trading days (starting Sept 3). 

They announced the will place an international capital raise (180M shares and up to 375M as optional) to institutional investors (Zurich trip, perhaps). The window will be Sept 9-11 and this will allow them to buy between 10K and 30K Bitcoin...in September alone.

Not to mention other catalyst which include that EVO sold 17.7M shares in two days this week, and it was alluded they sold another 10M last night. The BTC buys with those funds have NOT yet been announced.

And then you have our beloved preferreds, which will be voted on in the coming days. They have been paying down almost all their debt and now will have a huge BTC buy in Sept/Oct - this is the ideal situation to offer preferreds.

How can you not be ultra bullish on MetaPlanet??
 

📌 What MetaPlanet Announced
	•	Board Resolution (Aug 27, 2025): Approve issuance of new shares via international offering.
	•	Shares Offered:
	•	180M shares – firm underwriting.
	•	Up to 375M shares – optional, based on demand/market.
	•	Total max: 555M new shares.
	•	Markets: Overseas only (not Japan).
	•	U.S. sales limited to Qualified Institutional Buyers (Rule 144A).
	•	Underwriters: Morgan Stanley, Cantor Fitzgerald (joint bookrunners).

⸻

📌 Timing
	•	Pricing: Sept 9–11, 2025 (via bookbuilding).
	•	Settlement: Sept 16–18, 2025.
	•	Discount: Priced at 90–100% of TSE closing price at pricing.

⸻

📌 Dilution & Share Count
	•	Current shares (Aug 22, 2025): 722M.
	•	New shares issued: up to 555M.
	•	Post-issuance: ~1.277B shares.
	•	Dilution: ~43% for existing holders.

⸻

📌 Use of Proceeds (~¥130.3B net)
	1.	Purchase Bitcoin – ~¥123.8B (Sept–Oct 2025).
	2.	Bitcoin Income Generation (put option selling) – ~¥6.5B (Sept–Dec 2025).

⸻

📌 Lock-up
	•	CEO Simon Gerovich & MMXX Ventures (major shareholder) agreed to a 60-day lock-up (no insider sales without underwriter approval).
	•	Company also agreed not to issue/sell more stock during this period.

### Post #484: 2025-10-18 17:30 — `$MTPLF` `[Yolo]`

**MetaPlanet - Phase II**

Just an update on where I'm at.... still holding my 65,000+ shares. Many have sold... I get it. Do what you want.

But we're just about the start MetaPlanet's $MTPLF  **PHASE II**

1️⃣ **Core Engine – Bitcoin Treasury Operations**
This is the foundation of MetaPlanet. It’s where the company’s Bitcoin holdings live.

The focus here is accumulating BTC through treasury management using Perpetual Preferreds and common equity. 

2️⃣ **Internal – Bitcoin Income Generation**
This stage handles internal yield strategies like covered calls and options-based income on their BTC holdings.
It’s the engine that creates cash flow from Bitcoin without selling it

3️⃣ **Classified – Project NOVA**
It likely connects MetaPlanet’s internal financial engine to its external products.... possibly a proprietary financial technology, exchange, or custody infrastructure that could be developed in house that strengthens the ecosystem. I think of it as the “guidance system” of the rocket, steering everything toward higher Bitcoin efficiency and scalability. 

4️⃣ **Platform – Bitcoin.jp**
This is the public-facing platform that opens the gateway to Bitcoin in Japan.

It’s where users, institutions, and partners interact with MetaPlanet’s Bitcoin ecosystem. I believe this layer is the one that generates real revenue and brand presence, helping onboard the next wave of Bitcoin adoption in Asia.
